- **protect-commit-privacy**: Keeps the development conversation out of git history. Guard refuses `git commit` and `gh pr create|edit` whose message or body (inline -m/-b, -F/--file/--body-file, heredoc on -F -) holds a process-leak marker (session link, 'user asked', ...); commands behind cd, sh -c, sudo, env are read. Every number is derived from real data or cited to a source; an assumption is labeled as one. Use when a value was picked to look reasonable, a test went green by adjusting a constant, or a doc quotes a figure nobody re-measured. Do not use as a gate for explicit mechanical edits whose requested outcome does not depend on reproducing a defect.
